types = {
	TOP_infiltration_1 = {
		category = special_forces_raids
		custom_map_icon = GFX_raid_type_icon_direct_action_raid_supply
		days_to_prepare = 28
		days_re_enable = 30
		command_power = 20
		arrow = { type = line }
		visible = { TOP_enabled = yes check_variable = { TOP_case_phase^1 = 3 } OR = { check_variable = { TOP_case_method^1 = 3 } check_variable = { TOP_case_method^1 = 7 } } }
		show_target = { TOP_native_gate_1_3 = yes }
		available = {
			TOP_native_gate_1_3 = yes
			raid_available_base = yes
			raid_special_forces_tech_available = yes
		}
		launchable = { TOP_native_gate_1_3 = yes set_temp_variable = { cost = 150 } raid_can_launch = yes }
		target_type = { state = { always = yes } }
		unit_requirements = {
			battalion_types = { Special_Forces = { min = 2 } }
		}
		launch_sound = raid_launch_marine
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		starting_point = { types = { air_base naval_base } }
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = 0.25
				intel = { weight = 0.5 start_weight = -0.1 start_reference = 10 reference = 100 }
				experience = { weight = 0.5 start_weight = -0.25 reference = 0.75 }
				TOP_native_success_bonus = {
					scope = country
					formula = { base = 1 modifier = { factor = var:TOP_case_native_success_bonus^1 } }
					weight = 1
					reference = 100
					can_actor_affect = no
					can_target_affect = no
				}
				TOP_native_success_penalty = {
					scope = country
					formula = { base = 1 modifier = { factor = var:TOP_case_native_success_penalty^1 } }
					weight = -1
					reference = 100
					can_actor_affect = no
					can_target_affect = no
				}
				organisation = { reference = 100 weight = 0.2 start_weight = -0.1 }
				strength = { reference = 1 weight = 0.15 start_weight = -0.05 }
			}
			critical = { base = 0.05 }
			disaster = { base = 0.05 }
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				victim_effects = { send_raid_event_to_victim = yes }
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_pp_loss = -150 }
					apply_discounted_raid_pp_cost = yes
					raid_damage_units = { org_damage = 0.3 str_damage = 0.15 ratio = yes }
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.25
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_target = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_method = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_native_kind = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_tier = 0 }
					TOP_native_result_args = yes
				}
			}
			limited_success = {
				victim_effects = { send_raid_event_to_victim = yes }
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_pp_loss = -150 }
					apply_discounted_raid_pp_cost = yes
					raid_damage_units = { org_damage = 0.2 str_damage = 0.05 ratio = yes }
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.25
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_target = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_method = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_native_kind = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_tier = 1 }
					TOP_native_result_args = yes
				}
			}
			success = {
				victim_effects = { send_raid_event_to_victim = yes }
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_pp_loss = -150 }
					apply_discounted_raid_pp_cost = yes
					raid_damage_units = { org_damage = 0.2 str_damage = 0.02 ratio = yes }
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.25
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_target = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_method = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_native_kind = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_tier = 2 }
					TOP_native_result_args = yes
				}
			}
			critical_success = {
				victim_effects = { send_raid_event_to_victim = yes }
				actor_effects = {
					set_temp_variable = { raid_pp_loss = -150 }
					apply_discounted_raid_pp_cost = yes
					raid_damage_units = { org_damage = 0.1 str_damage = 0 ratio = yes }
					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.25
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_target = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_method = 3 }

			critical = { base = 0.05 }
			disaster = { base = 0.05 }
		}

			failure = {

					raid_add_unit_experience = 0.25
					set_temp_variable = { TOP_target = 6 }
